- **allele-based-read-scoring-and-haplotype-reweighting**: Use when raw long reads (Nanopore R9/R10 or PacBio CLR) from a diploid genome must be error-corrected where the sequencing error rate exceeds haplotype divergence and heterozygote alleles would otherwise be collapsed into the consensus. - **allele-depth-pileup-processing**: Use when shotgun metagenomic BAMs aligned to a reference gene database must be converted into allele-depth pile-ups (cellSNP VCF), clustered via MetaQuad, and normalized into per-gene nucleotide diversity for group comparisons. - **allele-diversity-integer-linear-programming**: Use when WGS reads have been mapped to a curated MLST allele database and you must infer, per locus, which alleles are present and their fractional proportions in mixed (within-host heterogeneous) samples. - **allele-fasta-sequence-processing**: Use when you have one FASTA file per locus with all identified alleles from a chewBBACA cg/wgMLST schema and need to assess allelic variability per locus via per-locus MSAs and phylogenetic trees. - **allele-frequency-binned-constraint-regression**: Use when gnomAD SNVs overlap fine-mapped eCLIP enriched windows and you need to test whether disruptive variants are depleted from factor-bound sequences as evidence of constraint. - **allele-frequency-density-clustering**: Use when you have cellsnp-lite AD/DP allele count matrices from shotgun metagenomic samples and need to identify shared informative variants versus background noise based on clustering of sample allele frequencies. - **allele-frequency-dissimilarity-computation**: Use when comparing treatment groups (e.g. CLA, LEVO vs OTHERS) at a time point such as T+6W using allele-frequency profiles of shared informative variants from a cellSNP-lite/MetaQuad pipeline on ARG VCF data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-dissimilarity-computation))
- **allele-frequency-extraction-from-vcf**: Use when VCF files from low-frequency variant calling on wastewater NGS samples must be matched against a lineage mutation profile (e.g., B.1.1.7 unique mutations via Pangolin rules or outbreak.info) and per-mutation allele frequencies or a non-zero minimum AF are needed. - **allele-frequency-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have vireoSNP clone labels and a set of MQuad ΔBIC-selected mtDNA variants and need to visually confirm clonal discrimination, e.g. reproducing Fig. 4c for MKN-45 scDNA-seq data. - **allele-frequency-matching-of-variant-sets**: Use when building labeled variant training sets for a variant-effect or disease-association classifier and you need negative controls frequency-matched to positives (MAF thresholds or matched MAF distributions) to avoid allele-frequency artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-frequency-matching-of-variant-sets))
- **allele-frequency-order-of-magnitude-comparison**: Use when comparing population allele frequencies (e.g., gnomAD AF) between the previous and current annotation snapshots of a submitted variant, to decide whether the shift is large enough to notify the user or should be silently ignored. - **allele-frequency-summary-statistics**: Use when you have VCF files of SARS-CoV-2 variants (e.g., from freebayes on wastewater NGS data) and need per-lineage abundance metrics such as mean allele frequency and variant counts. - **allele-frequency-threshold-classification**: Use when a resistance-calling pipeline must distinguish susceptible, low-frequency (minor), and high-frequency (major) resistant alleles from k-mer depths at mutation sites, and when a frequency threshold for calling resistance must be applied or justified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-threshold-classification))
- **allele-frequency-trajectory-simulation**: Use when the task requires simulating or visualizing how allele frequencies change through time at a biallelic locus under selection, drift, or their combination — e.g., reproducing dominance-regime dynamics (overdominance, underdominance, codominance) or Wright–Fisher drift-selection trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-trajectory-simulation))
- **allele-proportion-error-constraint-modeling**: Use when reconstructing a multi-sample strain decomposition from per-locus allele distributions (geneX_proportions.csv) where you must minimize previously unobserved strains while respecting allele proportions within a tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-proportion-error-constraint-modeling))
- **allele-proportion-estimation-from-read-mappings**: Use when processing WGS tick samples (2x76bp read pairs) against the 1726-allele B. burgdorferi MLST database and you need, per locus, the set of alleles and their fractional composition under within-host strain heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-proportion-estimation-from-read-mappings))
- **allele-sequence-similarity-pairing**: Use when you have called SV VCFs (e.g. from VolcanoSV, cuteSV, Sniffles2, pbsv, DeBreak) and a GIAB-style truth set, and need to measure how F1 depends on Truvari matching thresholds plus how accurately TP pairs recover breakpoints and allele sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-sequence-similarity-pairing))
- **allele-specific-cna-detection**: Use when analyzing PTA-amplified high-coverage (&gt;20x) scWGS BAMs with matched bulk WGS, to call small (200 kb–5 Mb) allele-specific CNAs that RDR-only callers miss and to obtain integer copy-number states per allele per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-cna-detection))
- **allele-specific-cna-detection-from-scrnaseq**: Use when the goal is to infer haplotype-aware CNA states or tumor subclones from single-cell RNA-seq count data (e.g., 10x Genomics droplet or Smart-seq2 tumor samples), especially when copy-neutral LOH or low tumor purity challenges expression-only methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-cna-detection-from-scrnaseq))
- **allele-specific-copy-number-inference**: Use when analyzing high-coverage (&gt;20x) PTA-amplified single-cell WGS data where small (100 kb–Mb scale) somatic CNAs must be called with allele-specific copy states, and paired bulk WGS is available for phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-copy-number-inference))
- **allele-specific-copy-number-state-inference**: Use when per-cell copy gain/loss/LOH states must be inferred from raw scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq counts (10x or Smart-seq2), especially for tumour subclone dissection or when benchmarking XClone against InferCNV/CopyKAT/CaSpER/Numbat with AUROC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-copy-number-state-inference))
- **allele-specific-expression-detection**: Use when multi-sample VCF files (GATK HaplotypeCaller output) from RNA-seq of heterozygous individuals are available and the goal is to identify which biallelic SNPs show statistically significant allele-specific expression per tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-expression-detection))
- **allele-specific-expression-gene-set-compilation**: Use when you have VADT sig_meta_analysis_variants.txt call sets for multiple tissues and need gene-level ASE sets, cross-tissue overlap percentages, or a high-ASE-score gene list for pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-expression-gene-set-compilation))
- **allele-specific-haplotype-assignment**: Use when ChIA-PET (or similar chromatin interaction) data must be resolved into haplotype-specific peaks and loops for a genome whose SNP phasing information is available, and allelic bias in binding coverage needs statistical testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-haplotype-assignment))
- **allele-specific-methylation-testing**: Use when haplotagged nanopore reads carry per-read mCG log-likelihood-ratio or 6mA-region calls and you need to find regions (CGIs, ChIP peaks) with statistically significant allele-specific methylation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-specific-methylation-testing))
- **allele-specific-methylation-visualization**: Use when WGBS reads have been aligned and methylation-called with Msuite and you need per-read, genotype-preserved methylation views at imprinted or allele-specific loci, e.g. to phase reads at a heterozygous A/G SNP and observe reciprocal methylation bars. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-methylation-visualization))
- **allele-specific-peptide-prioritization**: Use when patient-specific HLA allele lists (mhc-I.tsv / mhc-II.tsv) and per-variant-type variant_effects.tsv files are available and predicted neoantigens must be ranked per allele by MHC binding affinity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-peptide-prioritization))
- **allele-specific-pet-assignment**: Use when analyzing ChIA-PET bedpe data from a heterozygous sample (e.g., GM12878 with Illumina Platinum Genomes phased genotypes) to determine which parental allele mediates each chromatin loop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-pet-assignment))
- **allele-specific-read-counting**: Use when scRNA-Seq reads from a polymorphic F1 cross (e.g. CAST×B6) must be converted into per-allele gene counts, and you must decide between unique-read counting and multi-read weighted allocation before allelic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-read-counting))
- **allele-specific-read-partitioning**: Use when ONT reads (e.g., HG002 R10.4 BAM) must be partitioned into haplotype-specific subsets (HP:i:1 / HP:i:2) to enable allele-specific methylation calling downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-specific-read-partitioning))
- **allele-specific-short-read-alignment**: Use when scRNA-Seq reads from an F1 hybrid cross (e.g. CAST×B6) must be assigned to maternal/paternal alleles for allele-specific expression counting, and multi-mapping reads should be retained rather than discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-short-read-alignment))
- **allele-stratification-by-detection-efficiency**: Use when evaluating how per-allele data quality (fraction of detected targeted segments in chromatin tracing/FISH data) affects chromatin loop caller sensitivity or false-positive discovery, or when deciding a detection-efficiency cutoff before loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-stratification-by-detection-efficiency))
- **allele-umi-count-analysis**: Use when allele-level UMI counts from single-cell 3' RNA-seq (Cell Ranger output, SNVs called with Freebayes) must be converted into per-cell genotype calls or two-genotype mixture inference, e.g. donor/host chimerism in transplant or mixing datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-umi-count-analysis))
- **allele_count_analysis**: Run vcftools allele-count analysis and summarize polymorphic-site count using the built-in allele_count_analysis tool and bundled scripts. ([lukegood/EasyGS](https://github.com/lukegood/EasyGS/tree/HEAD/easygs/skills/allele_count_analysis) · ★10)
- **allele_frequency_analysis**: Run vcftools allele-frequency analysis and summarize polymorphic-site proportion using the built-in allele_frequency_analysis tool and bundled scripts. ([lukegood/EasyGS](https://github.com/lukegood/EasyGS/tree/HEAD/easygs/skills/allele_frequency_analysis) · ★10)
- **allele_frequency_spectrum_analysis**: Run PLINK allele-frequency spectrum analysis with the built-in allele_frequency_spectrum_analysis tool and bundled reporting scripts. ([lukegood/EasyGS](https://github.com/lukegood/EasyGS/tree/HEAD/easygs/skills/allele_frequency_spectrum_analysis) · ★10)
- **allelecall-scaling-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify the computational scaling of chewBBACA AlleleCall (runtime vs CPU count, per-sample seconds-to-minutes behaviour) for a fixed set of draft genome FASTA files on a laptop or HPC cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allelecall-scaling-analysis))
- **allelic-bursting-independence-testing**: Use when you have allele-specific single-cell count matrices and want to test per-gene whether maternal/paternal bursting events are independent (logOR ≈ 0), positively correlated, or anti-correlated, with FDR control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-bursting-independence-testing))
- **allelic-count-matrix-generation-from-bam**: Use when starting from cell-barcoded BAM/CRAM files from a scRNA-seq experiment (e.g., 10x Genomics) and you need allele-specific AD/DP matrices to feed haplotype-aware CNA callers such as XClone's BAF module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-count-matrix-generation-from-bam))
- **allelic-differential-splicing-statistical-modeling**: Use when merged per-allele inclusion and skipping counts (IJC/SJC for reference and alternative alleles across samples) are available and the goal is to call significant ASAS events at FDR ≤ 10% from replicate or population-scale RNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-differential-splicing-statistical-modeling))
- **allelic-dmr-detection**: Use when nanopore reads carry haplotype tags (maternal/paternal) plus per-read CpG methylation calls, and the goal is to identify allele-specific methylation or allelic DMRs between homologous chromosomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allelic-dmr-detection))
- **allelic-dropout-modeling-single-cell-wgs**: Use when a single-cell WGS CNA analysis must pick a per-dataset bin size that balances CNA detection resolution against amplification noise, e.g. before binning PTA- or MDA-amplified scWGS with HiScanner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-dropout-modeling-single-cell-wgs))
- **allelic-expression-state-classification**: Use when allelic read counts from single-cell RNA-Seq (e.g., CAST×B6 F1 embryos) need per-cell, per-gene P/B/M state calls, and random monoallelic bursts and sparse coverage make naive read-ratio thresholds unreliable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-expression-state-classification))
- **allelic-heterogeneity-multi-component-colocalization**: Use when multiple studies or tissues (e.g., eQTLGen and GTEx whole blood) have summary statistics or individual-level genotypes/phenotypes for the same locus and you need to test whether signals colocalize despite possible multiple causal alleles and LD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-heterogeneity-multi-component-colocalization))
- **allelic-profile-matrix-generation**: Use when draft/complete bacterial genome FASTA assemblies must be converted into a gene-by-gene allelic profile matrix against a pre-defined cgMLST/wgMLST schema, e.g. for strain typing or outbreak analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allelic-profile-matrix-generation))
- **allelic-proportion-partial-pooling**: Use when single-cell allele-specific RNA-Seq read counts are too sparse (e.g. &lt;10 unique reads per gene) for reliable per-cell allelic proportion estimates, and classification of P/B/M expression states is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-proportion-partial-pooling))
- **allelic-read-uniqueness-qc-filtering**: Use when RNA-seq reads aligned to two haplotype-modified personal genomes must be allele-assigned: filter multi-mapping or between-genome discordant reads, then assign haplotypes by SNP majority voting before ASAS counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-read-uniqueness-qc-filtering))

- **alleypin-pinfriends**: AlleyPin 品牌 IP「PinFriends」七隻角色（山豆／山豆醫師、吐司（設計稿寫土司）、水可、伊吉、瑞比、波提、石子）的外型規格、精確色票、高矮胖瘦比例與參考圖包，用來叫 Codex imagegen（或任何生圖工具）畫出不走樣的角色圖。只要任務要「生成、畫、做」任何含這些角色的圖——KV、主視覺、社群首圖、貼圖、表情包、簡報插圖、landing page 角色、角色合照、新動作、新服裝——或使用者提到 PinFriends、AlleyPin IP、品牌吉祥物、山豆熊、鴨子吐司、兔子瑞比、獅子波提、企鵝伊吉、河童水可，就一定要先用這個 skill，即使他只說「幫我生一張山豆拿手機的圖」。也用在檢查生成出來的角色圖像不像、哪裡走樣。不適用：只是從現成素材庫挑一張既有 IP 圖（那是 alleypin-slide-design／social-post-generator 的素材庫工作）。 ([AlleyPin/alleypin-pinfriends](https://github.com/AlleyPin/alleypin-pinfriends/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
